715: # every fresh and resumed dispatch.
716: #
717: # WHAT IT PROVES: the two-HEAD gate proves committed-HEAD freshness.
718: # Non-HEAD inputs are bound in the constructed mirror's manifest AT
719: # CONSTRUCTION TIME, and changes after construction are caught by TWO
720: # status-based fingerprints, one per side. The source-status comparison
721: # catches source-side drift WHEN IT IS VISIBLE TO IT: changes that move
722: # the status listing, or that alter the content of a path the listing
723: # names. The mirror-state comparison catches the same class of change
724: # made directly INSIDE the mirror after construction - an uncommitted
725: # edit to a file the mirror's own HEAD still calls clean, or a file
726: # added to the mirror - which the two-HEAD gate cannot see because
727: # neither head moves. A tracked file git reports CLEAN, on either side,
728: # is covered by neither fingerprint, so a raw-byte change surviving the
729: # clean filter unchanged is NOT covered. The contract region carries the
730: # same qualification; this comment said "are detected" flat until round
731: # 3 of the mode-diff debate pointed out that the code and the contract
732: # had drifted apart again, in the direction of the code claiming more.
733: #
734: # Two further refusals guard the comparison itself, both ahead of any
735: # digest work: the source and the mirror must not be the same directory
736: # - otherwise every remaining comparison is trivially satisfied whenever
737: # the two heads already agree, which they do whenever the mirror needed
738: # no remediation commit - and the live -MirrorPath must canonically
739: # match -ExpectedMirrorPath. Both are CALLER ARGUMENTS in the SAME
740: # invocation rather than values read back from the construction record,
741: # so the guarantee is narrow: it catches a verify whose two path
742: # arguments disagree, and it does NOT refuse a mirror rebuilt somewhere
743: # else with both arguments updated to the new path.
744: #
745: # Neither status comparison is decoration. An edit to an untracked or
746: # ignored review input moves NEITHER head, and that content class is
747: # precisely what the mirror exists to carry, so a gate built only on
748: # HEADs would be blind in the middle of the feature.
749: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
750: if ($VerifyIdentity) {
751:     # Resolve THROUGH THE PROVIDER here too. Build does this below and
752:     # verify did not, so a relative -MirrorPath resolved against the
753:     # process location for Test-Path and against the provider location
754:     # for git - the same divergence this file's own history block calls
755:     # an ordinary PowerShell condition. Verify only reads, so the
756:     # realistic failure was a spurious block rather than a wrong pass,
757:     # but a tool should not teach one lesson in one mode and forget it
758:     # in the other.
759:     $MirrorPath = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($MirrorPath)
760:     $ExpectedMirrorPath = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($ExpectedMirrorPath)
761:     $shaRx = '^[0-9a-f]{40}$'
762:     if ($SourceHead -cnotmatch $shaRx) {
763:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the recorded source head is missing or not a" +
764:             " commit id, so nothing was compared")
765:         exit 1
766:     }
767:     if ($MirrorHead -cnotmatch $shaRx) {
768:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the recorded mirror head is missing or not a" +
769:             " commit id, so nothing was compared")
770:         exit 1
771:     }
772:     if ($SourceStatusSha256 -cnotmatch '^[0-9a-f]{64}$') {
773:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the recorded source status hash is missing or" +
774:             " malformed, so nothing was compared")
775:         exit 1
776:     }
777:     if ($MirrorStateSha256 -cnotmatch '^[0-9a-f]{64}$') {
778:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the recorded mirror state hash is missing or" +
779:             " malformed, so nothing was compared")
780:         exit 1
781:     }
782: 
783:     # BEFORE ANY DIGEST WORK. A source pointed at itself as the mirror
784:     # would satisfy every remaining comparison trivially whenever the
785:     # two heads already agree, which they do whenever the mirror needed
786:     # no remediation commit.
787:     $cmp = [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
788:     $rrNorm = $RepoRoot.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
789:     $mpNorm = $MirrorPath.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
790:     if ($mpNorm.Equals($rrNorm, $cmp)) {
791:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source and the mirror are the same" +
792:             " directory ($MirrorPath), so nothing was compared")
793:         exit 1
794:     }
795:     # The live -MirrorPath must canonically match -ExpectedMirrorPath.
796:     # Both are caller arguments in this same invocation, so this catches
797:     # a call whose two path arguments disagree. It does NOT refuse a
798:     # mirror rebuilt elsewhere with both arguments updated.
799:     $expNorm = $ExpectedMirrorPath.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
800:     if (-not $mpNorm.Equals($expNorm, $cmp)) {
801:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: -MirrorPath ($MirrorPath) does not match" +
802:             " -ExpectedMirrorPath ($ExpectedMirrorPath) in this same call")
803:         exit 1
804:     }
805: 
806:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $MirrorPath -PathType Container)) {
807:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror at $MirrorPath is gone, so its" +
808:             " identity could not be measured")
809:         exit 1
810:     }
811: 
812:     $liveSource = Get-HeadSha $RepoRoot
813:     if (-not $liveSource.Ok) {
814:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $liveSource.Reason + " - an unread source" +
815:             " head is never a matching one")
816:         exit 1
817:     }
818:     if ($liveSource.Sha -ne $SourceHead) {
819:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source head moved since construction" +
820:             " (recorded " + $SourceHead + ", live " + $liveSource.Sha +
821:             ") - rebuild the mirror")
822:         exit 1
823:     }
824: 
825:     $liveMirror = Get-HeadSha $MirrorPath
826:     if (-not $liveMirror.Ok) {
827:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $liveMirror.Reason + " - an unread mirror" +
828:             " head is never a matching one")
829:         exit 1
830:     }
831:     if ($liveMirror.Sha -ne $MirrorHead) {
832:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror head changed since construction" +
833:             " (recorded " + $MirrorHead + ", live " + $liveMirror.Sha +
834:             ") - something wrote into the mirror")
835:         exit 1
836:     }
837: 
838:     $liveStatus = Get-StatusSha256 $RepoRoot
839:     if (-not $liveStatus.Ok) {
840:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source status could not be captured (" +
841:             $liveStatus.Reason + ") - an unmade measurement is never a clean" +
842:             " one")
843:         exit 1
844:     }
845:     if ($liveStatus.Sha -ne $SourceStatusSha256) {
846:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source status changed since construction" +
847:             " - a tracked, untracked or ignored review input moved without" +
848:             " moving either head, so the mirror no longer carries what the" +
849:             " source holds. Rebuild the mirror.")
850:         exit 1
851:     }
852: 
853:     # THE MIRROR-SIDE FINGERPRINT, recomputed live. Catches an
854:     # uncommitted edit to a file the mirror's own HEAD still calls clean,
855:     # or a file added to the mirror after construction - the case the
856:     # two-HEAD gate cannot see because neither head moves for it.
857:     $liveMirrorState = Get-StatusSha256 $MirrorPath
858:     if (-not $liveMirrorState.Ok) {
859:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror's current state could not be" +
860:             " measured (" + $liveMirrorState.Reason + ") - an unmade" +
861:             " measurement is never a clean one")
862:         exit 1
863:     }
864:     if ($liveMirrorState.Sha -ne $MirrorStateSha256) {
865:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror's contents changed since" +
866:             " construction - something edited or added a file inside the" +
867:             " mirror without moving its head. Rebuild the mirror.")
868:         exit 1
869:     }
870: 
871:     Write-Output "identity: verified"
872:     exit 0
873: }
874: 
875: # RESOLVE ONCE, THROUGH THE PROVIDER, BEFORE ANY GUARD. An earlier
876: # revision guarded `[IO.Path]::GetFullPath($MirrorPath)`, which resolves a
877: # relative path against the PROCESS working directory, while `Test-Path`
878: # and `Remove-Item -Recurse -Force` further down resolve the same parameter
879: # against PowerShell's PROVIDER location. When a caller's two locations
880: # differ - any in-session use after Set-Location - the guard approved one
881: # absolute target and the recursive delete destroyed another. Found by the
882: # mode-diff review, 2026-07-28; a prior review had called it theoretical,
883: # which was wrong: -MirrorPath is a public parameter and the divergence is
884: # an ordinary PowerShell condition. From here down, only the resolved
885: # values are used.
886: $MirrorPath = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($MirrorPath)
887: if ($OverrideOut) {
888:     $OverrideOut = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($OverrideOut)
889: }
890: 
891: # -EXTRAINPUT, PARSED BY HAND out of the remaining-argument catch-all.
892: # PowerShell's own binder refuses to bind one named parameter twice, even
893: # to an array, so a literal repeatable `-ExtraInput <path>` cannot be a
894: # declared parameter. Anything left in $BuildRemainingArgs that is not an
895: # "-ExtraInput <value>" pair is a parameter this tool does not have -
896: # this is also what refuses a re-mint or reseal flag: there is
897: # deliberately no such mode, so any flag claiming to be one lands here
898: # and is rejected the same way an unrecognized parameter always is.
899: $ExtraInputPaths = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
900: # Measured on both hosts: when NOTHING would otherwise land in a
901: # ValueFromRemainingArguments array, -File invocation still hands back
902: # one PHANTOM $null element instead of a genuinely empty array. Neither
903: # an -ExtraInput flag name nor a real value is ever $null or empty, so
904: # filtering both out removes exactly the phantom and nothing a real
905: # caller could have meant.
906: $remaining = @($BuildRemainingArgs | Where-Object { $_ })
907: $ri = 0
908: while ($ri -lt $remaining.Count) {
909:     $tok = [string]$remaining[$ri]
910:     if ($tok -cne "-ExtraInput") {
911:         Write-Output ("ERROR: unrecognized parameter '" + $tok + "'")
912:         exit 2
913:     }
914:     if ($ri + 1 -ge $remaining.Count) {
915:         Write-Output "ERROR: -ExtraInput requires a value"
916:         exit 2
917:     }
918:     $eiPath = [string]$remaining[$ri + 1]
919:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $eiPath -PathType Leaf)) {
920:         Write-Output ("ERROR: -ExtraInput '" + $eiPath + "' is not a file")
921:         exit 2
922:     }
923:     [void]$ExtraInputPaths.Add((Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $eiPath).Path)
924:     $ri += 2
925: }
926: 
927: # OVERLAP GUARD, before anything is created or deleted. -Force recursively
928: # deletes MirrorPath, so a MirrorPath equal to, inside, or containing
929: # RepoRoot would destroy the user's working tree. robocopy over an
930: # overlapping pair is equally unsafe. This runs FIRST, because by the time
931: # Remove-Item runs it is too late to check.
932: $rr = $RepoRoot.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
933: $mp = $MirrorPath.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
934: $cmp = [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
935: if ($mp.Equals($rr, $cmp)) {
936:     Write-Output "ERROR: the mirror path is the repo root itself"
937:     exit 2
938: }
939: if ($mp.StartsWith($rr, $cmp)) {
940:     Write-Output ("ERROR: the mirror path is inside the repo ($MirrorPath)" +
941:         " - building or forcing there would write into, or delete, the" +
942:         " tree under review")
943:     exit 2
944: }
945: if ($rr.StartsWith($mp, $cmp)) {
946:     Write-Output ("ERROR: the mirror path contains the repo ($MirrorPath)" +
947:         " - -Force would delete the repo with it")
948:     exit 2
949: }
950: 
951: # Resolve the EFFECTIVE override path here, default included, and guard it
952: # beside the mirror guard. Deferring the default until after the mirror is
953: # built, copied, remediated and manifested would mean discovering a stale
954: # or overlapping artifact only after all that work had already happened,
955: # and -SkipProbe would bypass the check entirely.
956: if (-not $OverrideOut) {
957:     $OverrideOut = Join-Path (Split-Path $MirrorPath -Parent) `
958:         ((Split-Path $MirrorPath -Leaf) + ".skills-override.txt")
959: }
960: $op = $OverrideOut.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
961: foreach ($protected in @($rr, $mp)) {
962:     if (($op + "/").Equals($protected, $cmp) -or
963:         ($op + "/").StartsWith($protected, $cmp) -or
964:         $protected.StartsWith($op + "/", $cmp)) {
965:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the override path overlaps a protected tree" +
966:             " ($OverrideOut)")
967:         exit 2
968:     }
969: }
970: if (Test-Path $OverrideOut) {
971:     Write-Output ("ERROR: $OverrideOut already exists - a stale override" +
972:         " reads exactly like a fresh one")
973:     exit 2
974: }
975: 
976: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
977: # PATH BUDGET PRE-FLIGHT. Runs here, after the overlap and override
978: # guards and BEFORE anything is created or deleted, because the failure
979: # it prevents is a MID-COPY one: robocopy stops at the first destination
980: # it cannot create, leaving a partially populated mirror that reads
981: # exactly like a complete one.
982: #
983: # THE UNIVERSE, frozen text: every file and directory destination that
984: # the exact `robocopy /E` operation may create beneath the resolved
985: # mirror root, including tracked, untracked, ignored, and all `.git`
986: # content. Two consequences that narrower readings get wrong - a
987: # directory with no files in it is still a destination, and `.git` is
988: # copied so `.git` counts.
989: #
990: # THE LIMIT is 260 characters as a conservative policy across both
991: # supported PowerShell hosts. It is a deterministic refusal threshold,
992: # not a claim about the maximum any host, API, OS configuration, or
993: # downstream client could support.
994: #
995: # THE ARITHMETIC is resolved mirror-root length, plus separator, plus
996: # the relative destination path length.
997: $PathBudget = 260
998: $budgetRoot = $MirrorPath.TrimEnd("\", "/")
999: $budgetRootLen = $budgetRoot.Length
1000: 
1001: # -OverrideOut is written BESIDE the mirror by this script, not by
1002: # robocopy, so the copy universe never covers it. Its own check or none.
1003: if ($OverrideOut.Length -ge $PathBudget) {
1004:     Write-Output ("ERROR: path budget exceeded by the override file - " +
1005:         "$OverrideOut is $($OverrideOut.Length) characters and the limit " +
1006:         "is $PathBudget")
1007:     exit 2
1008: }
1009: 
1010: if ($budgetRootLen -ge $PathBudget) {
1011:     Write-Output ("ERROR: path budget exceeded by the mirror root alone - " +
1012:         "root is $budgetRootLen characters and the limit is $PathBudget")
1013:     exit 2
1014: }
1015: 
1016: $srcRoot = $RepoRoot.TrimEnd("\", "/")
1017: # The prefix stripped to form a relative path, rebuilt rather than
1018: # derived from a length. A drive root trims to "C:", and "C:".Length + 1
1019: # is 3 only by coincidence of that one case; on any root ending in a
1020: # colon the arithmetic silently under-measures every relative path,
1021: # which is the permissive direction.
1022: $srcPrefix = $srcRoot
1023: if (-not $srcPrefix.EndsWith("\")) { $srcPrefix = $srcPrefix + "\" }
1024: 
1025: # The ROOT is a source path too. Checking only entries below it leaves
1026: # the one reparse point most likely to exist unmeasured, and the
1027: # contract clause says "a source reparse point" with no exemption.
1028: try {
1029:     $rootAttr = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($srcRoot)
1030: } catch {
1031:     Write-Output ("ERROR: $srcRoot could not be enumerated, so the path " +
1032:         "budget was never measured: " + $_.Exception.Message)
1033:     exit 2
1034: }
1035: if (($rootAttr -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0) {
1036:     Write-Output ("ERROR: $srcRoot is a reparse point - the mirror refuses " +
1037:         "to measure or copy across one")
1038:     exit 2
1039: }
1040: 
1041: $deepestLen = -1
1042: $deepestRel = ""
1043: $dirAttr = [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory
1044: $reparseAttr = [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint
1045: 
1046: # Every directory link target this walk has already followed. Case
1047: # insensitive because the filesystem is.
1048: $followedTargets = New-Object "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" (
1049:     [System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)
1050: 
1051: # Every directory link the walk meets that is NOT itself behind another
1052: # link: its relative path under the source root and its resolved
1053: # absolute target. The copy runs with /XJD and creates nothing beneath a
1054: # link, so a link is ONE destination and the entries beneath it are not
1055: # measured against the budget. The walk still DESCENDS through it,
1056: # because the cycle refusal and the overlap refusal apply to every link
1057: # the manifest can reach, and a link behind a link is reachable; a walk
1058: # that stopped at the outer link never saw an inner cycle (cross-vendor
1059: # round 1, 2026-09-05). A link behind a link is not recorded: it exists
1060: # in the target and is reached through the outer junction.
1061: $sourceLinks = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
1062: 
1063: $pending = New-Object System.Collections.Stack
1064: $pending.Push(@{ Path = $srcRoot; UnderLink = $false })
1065: while ($pending.Count -gt 0) {
1066:     $item = $pending.Pop()
1067:     $dir = $item.Path
1068:     $underLink = $item.UnderLink
1069:     $entries = $null
1070:     try {
1071:         $entries = @([System.IO.Directory]::GetFileSystemEntries($dir))
1072:     } catch {
1073:         # An unreadable path BLOCKS. It is never skipped: a path that
1074:         # cannot be measured is not a path known to fit, and the same
1075:         # hole semantics the manifest builder states apply here.
1076:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $dir could not be enumerated, so the path " +
1077:             "budget was never measured: " + $_.Exception.Message)
1078:         exit 2
1079:     }
1080:     foreach ($entry in $entries) {
1081:         $attr = 0
1082:         try {
1083:             $attr = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($entry)
1084:         } catch {
1085:             Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry could not be enumerated, so the " +
1086:                 "path budget was never measured: " + $_.Exception.Message)
1087:             exit 2
1088:         }
1089:         # A source directory reparse point is NOT copied through: the
1090:         # copy runs with /XJD, which excludes directory junctions and
1091:         # directory symbolic links, so the link is one destination and
1092:         # nothing beneath it is. The re-link step after the final sweep
1093:         # re-creates it as a junction. The walk still enters it, for the
1094:         # two refusals below only: measured 2026-09-05, the manifest
1095:         # expansion starts a listing at every link it finds, so a link
1096:         # onto one of its own ancestors or a tree whose links reach one
1097:         # target twice must be refused wherever it sits.
1098:         $isDirLink = $false
1099:         if (($attr -band $reparseAttr) -ne 0 -and ($attr -band $dirAttr) -ne 0) {
1100:             $isDirLink = $true
1101:             # git's optional index refresh writes the repository's own
1102:             # .git/index during every status capture. That is a write
1103:             # into the repository and never through a link only while
1104:             # .git is not itself a link, so one is refused here rather
1105:             # than assumed away (cross-vendor round 2, 2026-09-05).
1106:             if ([System.IO.Path]::GetFileName($entry) -ieq ".git") {
1107:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry - .git is a directory link, and" +
1108:                     " the status captures write the repository's own index," +
1109:                     " so this tree cannot be measured without writing" +
1110:                     " through a link")
1111:                 exit 2
1112:             }
1113:             $target = $null
1114:             try {
1115:                 $item = Get-Item -LiteralPath $entry -Force -ErrorAction Stop
1116:                 $target = $item.Target
1117:                 # Windows PowerShell 5.1 hands back a string COLLECTION here
1118:                 # and PowerShell 7 hands back a plain string, so take the
1119:                 # first element of anything that is not already a string
1120:                 # rather than testing for one collection type.
1121:                 if ($null -ne $target -and $target -isnot [string]) {
1122:                     foreach ($candidate in $target) { $target = $candidate; break }
1123:                 }
1124:             } catch {
1125:                 $target = $null
1126:             }
1127:             if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($target)) {
1128:                 # An unresolvable link is the same class as an unreadable
1129:                 # directory: a path that cannot be measured is not a path
1130:                 # known to fit.
1131:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry is a directory reparse point " +
1132:                     "whose target could not be read, so the path budget was " +
1133:                     "never measured")
1134:                 exit 2
1135:             }
1136: 
1137:             $entryFull = ""
1138:             $targetFull = ""
1139:             try {
1140:                 $entryFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($entry)
1141:                 # A RELATIVE TARGET IS RELATIVE TO THE LINK, NOT TO US.
1142:                 # `GetFullPath` with one argument resolves against the
1143:                 # PROCESS working directory, so a relative symbolic link
1144:                 # target resolved to wherever this script happened to be
1145:                 # invoked from: two distinct targets could compare equal
1146:                 # and read as a repeat, and a genuine self-cycle could
1147:                 # compare against the wrong absolute path and be missed.
1148:                 # Junctions always store an absolute target, which is why
1149:                 # the junction tests did not show it; symbolic links do
1150:                 # not. Combine-then-normalize rather than the two-argument
1151:                 # overload, which .NET Framework 4.8 does not carry.
1152:                 if ([System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($target)) {
1153:                     $targetFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($target)
1154:                 } else {
1155:                     $linkParent = [System.IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($entryFull)
1156:                     $targetFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath(
1157:                         [System.IO.Path]::Combine($linkParent, $target))
1158:                 }
1159:             } catch {
1160:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry is a directory reparse point " +
1161:                     "whose target " + $target + " is not a usable path, so " +
1162:                     "the path budget was never measured")
1163:                 exit 2
1164:             }
1165:             $targetPrefix = $targetFull.TrimEnd("\") + "\"
1166:             if ($entryFull.Equals($targetFull, "OrdinalIgnoreCase") -or
1167:                 $entryFull.StartsWith($targetPrefix, "OrdinalIgnoreCase")) {
1168:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry points at $targetFull, which " +
1169:                     "contains it - following that link would never " +
1170:                     "terminate, so the mirror refuses it")
1171:                 exit 2
1172:             }
1173:             if (-not $followedTargets.Add($targetFull)) {
1174:                 # Two links onto one target is not itself a cycle, but it is
1175:                 # indistinguishable from one here without walking the whole
1176:                 # graph, and refusing is the direction that cannot
1177:                 # mismeasure.
1178:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry points at $targetFull, which " +
1179:                     "another link in this tree already reaches - the mirror " +
1180:                     "refuses a tree whose links overlap")
1181:                 exit 2
1182:             }
1183:             if (-not $underLink) {
1184:                 [void]$sourceLinks.Add(@{ Rel = $entry.Substring($srcPrefix.Length)
1185:                                           Target = $targetFull })
1186:             }
1187:         }
1188:         # Only a destination counts. The link itself is one; nothing
1189:         # beneath any link is created by the copy.
1190:         if (-not $underLink) {
1191:             $rel = $entry.Substring($srcPrefix.Length)
1192:             if ($rel.Length -gt $deepestLen) {
1193:                 $deepestLen = $rel.Length
1194:                 $deepestRel = $rel
1195:             }
1196:         }
1197:         if (($attr -band $dirAttr) -ne 0) {
1198:             $pending.Push(@{ Path = $entry; UnderLink = ($underLink -or $isDirLink) })
1199:         }
1200:     }
1201: }
1202: 
1203: if ($deepestLen -ge 0) {
1204:     $deepestSum = $budgetRootLen + 1 + $deepestLen
1205:     if ($deepestSum -ge $PathBudget) {
1206:         Write-Output ("ERROR: path budget exceeded - mirror root is " +
1207:             "$budgetRootLen characters, the deepest relative destination " +
1208:             "is $deepestLen characters, their sum is $deepestSum, and the " +
1209:             "limit is $PathBudget. Build the mirror at a shorter path. " +
1210:             "Deepest: $deepestRel")
1211:         exit 2
1212:     }
1213: }
1214: 
1215: # LINK TARGETS ARE PROTECTED TREES. The overlap guard above compares the
1216: # mirror and override paths against the source root only, and a link
1217: # target lives outside it. Cross-vendor round 1 (2026-09-05) named the
1218: # case: -MirrorPath at a link's target with -Force would delete the
1219: # user's reference checkout, and -OverrideOut inside it would write
1220: # there. Round 2 added two more: an INNER link's target is reached by
1221: # the manifest just the same, so every target the walk followed is
1222: # protected, not only the recorded outer links; and a path spelled
1223: # THROUGH some other link can land inside a protected target without
1224: # matching its spelling, so a mirror or override path with a reparse
1225: # point among its existing ancestors is refused outright rather than
1226: # compared. This runs before anything is created or deleted.
1227: function Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink($path) {
1228:     # The path itself, then each existing ancestor up to the drive root:
1229:     # is any of them a reparse point? Attributes are read directly
1230:     # rather than after a Test-Path, because a DANGLING junction is a
1231:     # reparse point Test-Path may report as absent; a missing entry is
1232:     # the one condition that skips a level, and it is recognised by the
1233:     # exception type, never by a false from a helper. Returns the
1234:     # offending path or $null.
1235:     $probe = ([string]$path).TrimEnd("\", "/")
1236:     while ($probe -and -not [string]::IsNullOrEmpty([System.IO.Path]::GetFileName($probe))) {
1237:         $pa = 0
1238:         $missing = $false
1239:         try {
1240:             $pa = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($probe)
1241:         } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
1242:             $missing = $true
1243:         } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
1244:             $missing = $true
1245:         } catch {
1246:             Write-Output ("ERROR: " + $probe + " could not be read while" +
1247:                 " checking for a directory link: " + $_.Exception.Message)
1248:             exit 2
1249:         }
1250:         if (-not $missing -and (($pa -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0)) {
1251:             return $probe
1252:         }
1253:         $probe = [System.IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($probe)
1254:     }
1255:     return $null
1256: }
1257: 
1258: foreach ($pair in @(@("mirror path", $MirrorPath), @("override path", $OverrideOut))) {
1259:     $label = $pair[0]
1260:     $hit = Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink $pair[1]
1261:     if ($hit) {
1262:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the " + $label + " passes through a directory" +
1263:             " link at " + $hit + " - a path reached through a link can alias" +
1264:             " a tree the mirror only links to, so the mirror refuses it")
1265:         exit 2
1266:     }
1267: }
1268: # A followed TARGET that is itself a link, or sits beneath one, is the
1269: # alias in the other direction (cross-vendor round 3): the walk records
1270: # the target as spelled, so a mirror path at the real directory behind
1271: # it neither passes through a link nor overlaps the recorded text.
1272: foreach ($target in @($followedTargets)) {
1273:     $hit = Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink $target
1274:     if ($hit) {
1275:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the source link target " + $target + " is" +
1276:             " itself a directory link or sits beneath one (" + $hit + ") -" +
1277:             " the mirror cannot protect a tree it cannot name, so it" +
1278:             " refuses to build")
1279:         exit 2
1280:     }
1281: }
1282: foreach ($target in @($followedTargets)) {
1283:     $tp = ([string]$target).Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
1284:     foreach ($pair in @(@("mirror path", $mp), @("override path", ($op + "/")))) {
1285:         $label = $pair[0]
1286:         $cand = $pair[1]
1287:         if ($cand.Equals($tp, $cmp) -or $cand.StartsWith($tp, $cmp) -or
1288:             $tp.StartsWith($cand, $cmp)) {
1289:             Write-Output ("ERROR: the " + $label + " overlaps the target of a" +
1290:                 " source link (" + $target + ") - building there would write" +
1291:                 " into, or delete, a tree the mirror only links to")
1292:             exit 2
1293:         }
1294:     }
1295: }

1342: # THE THREE TEST SEAMS, and the rule they must satisfy LITERALLY. Two of
1343: # them are declared here; the third, $seamFailLinkTarget, is declared at
1344: # the top of the execution section because Get-FilesBeneath reads it
1345: # during the source status capture ABOVE, and it only ever ADDS the
1346: # link-target resolution failure inside that expansion.
1347: #
1348: # Each is a BOOLEAN that ORs one extra block condition into a comparison
1349: # below. None can supply a value, so none can suppress a real
1350: # measurement, and setting one can only ever ADD a reason to fail.
1351: # That makes the one-way property structural rather than a property of
1352: # whatever value someone happens to pass.
1353: #
1354: # TWO earlier shapes failed that bar, and both were caught by review
1355: # rather than by reasoning, which is why the property is now asserted by
1356: # a test instead of argued in a comment:
1357: #
1358: #   1. A copy-source override aimed at a same-HEAD tree with different
1359: #      ignored content would have BUILT, carrying the wrong non-HEAD
1360: #      content under a record attesting the real source. Its partner
1361: #      committed into $RepoRoot, contradicting this tool's promise three
1362: #      lines from the top that it never writes to the real tree.
1363: #   2. The replacement INJECTED a value. A parent that set it to the
1364: #      repo's own current HEAD - trivially readable in advance -
1365: #      suppressed the genuine post-copy measurement and turned a build
1366: #      that should have failed into one that passed. That is the exact
1367: #      inverse of the claim the comment was making.
1368: #
1369: # Any parent process can set these variables, so the shape has to be
1370: # safe on its own, not safe because no shipped caller sets it.
1371: $seamFailSourceStable = [bool]$env:PARALLAX_MIRROR_SEAM_FAIL_SOURCE_STABLE

1688:         " quarantine every round of the review that follows, or absorb" +
1689:         " changes it should have caught.")
1690:     exit 1
1691: }
1692: $parsed = ConvertTo-StatusRecord $captured.Fields
1693: if ($parsed.ContainsKey("Error")) {
1694:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $parsed.Error)
1695:     exit 1
1696: }
1697: $baseline = @(@($parsed.Records) |
1698:     ForEach-Object { Format-StatusRecord $_ })
1699: $subjects = Get-ManifestSubject $parsed.Records
1700: if ($subjects.ContainsKey("Error")) {
1701:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $subjects.Error)
1702:     exit 1
1703: }
1704: $manifestResult = Get-ContentManifest $MirrorPath $subjects.Paths
1705: if ($manifestResult.ContainsKey("Error")) {
1706:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $manifestResult.Error)
1707:     exit 1
1708: }
1709: $manifest = $manifestResult.Paths
1710: 
1711: # ONE digest over the baseline just captured and the manifest just
1712: # built, so it describes exactly the tree those two printed sections
1713: # describe - not a second, separately-timed measurement of the mirror
1714: # that could disagree with what was printed above it.
1715: $mirrorStateSha256 = Get-CombinedSha256 $captured.Fields $manifest
1716: 
1717: $probeLine = "skipped"
1718: $overrideFile = ""
1719: if (-not $SkipProbe) {
1720:     # $OverrideOut was resolved and guarded at the top. The probe's
1721:     # verified value IS the dispatch's input, so a mirror built without it
1722:     # leaves the transport with a file that does not exist.
1723:     $probeScript = Join-Path (Split-Path $PSCommandPath -Parent) "codex-context-probe.ps1"
1724:     $hostExe = (Get-Process -Id $PID).Path

1726:         -WorkDir $MirrorPath -SuppressSkills -OverrideOut $OverrideOut `
1727:         -Json -CodexCommand $CodexCommand

1729:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the client context probe did not pass: " +
1730:             (@($probeOut) -join "`n"))
1731:         exit 1
1732:     }
1733:     $probeLine = (@($probeOut) -join "`n").Trim()
1734:     $overrideFile = $OverrideOut
1735: }
1736: 
1737: Write-Output ("mirror: " + $MirrorPath)
1738: # TWO identities, not one. They differ whenever remediation committed,
1739: # which is the ordinary case for a repo carrying a tracked back-channel,
1740: # so a record printing one of them twice would be wrong in the common
1741: # case rather than the rare one.
1742: Write-Output ("source_head: " + $sourceBefore.Sha)
1743: Write-Output ("mirror_head: " + $head)
1744: Write-Output ("source_status_sha256: " + $sourceStatus.Sha)
1745: Write-Output ("mirror_state_sha256: " + $mirrorStateSha256)
1746: Write-Output "baseline:"
1747: foreach ($b in $baseline) { Write-Output ("  " + $b) }
1748: Write-Output "manifest:"
1749: foreach ($m in $manifest) { Write-Output ("  " + $m) }
1750: Write-Output "links:"
1751: foreach ($lnk in @($sourceLinks)) {
1752:     Write-Output ("  " + $lnk.Rel + " -> " + $lnk.Target)
1753: }

1755: Write-Output ("override: " + $overrideFile)

1757: # must not share its exit code with one that is. -SkipProbe exists for
1758: # offline construction and for the tests; it is not a way to reach a clean
1759: # outcome without a measurement. Found by the mode-diff review, 2026-07-28.
1760: if ($SkipProbe) {

1762:         " this mirror is not cleared for dispatch and carries no verified" +

 succeeded in 185ms:
1: # dispatch-round.ps1 - prepare a completion-coupled review-round dispatch,
2: # and classify a finished one: build the round's directory as one
3: # fail-closed transaction and print the exact command line the caller
4: # runs as a named background task; later, redeem that round's
5: # reservation and report the one state its dispatch directory is in.
6: #
7: # TWO MODES:
8: #
9: #   Prepare:  -DispatchDir <path> -WrapperBody <path> -ReceiptPath <path>
10: #             -Round <label> -WorkingDirectory <path> -RepoRoot <path>
11: #             -SourceHead <sha> -MirrorHead <sha> -SourceStatusSha256 <hex>
12: #             -MirrorStateSha256 <hex> -ExpectedMirrorPath <path>
13: #             -DispatchHost <pwsh|powershell> -PriorStateFile <path>
14: #             (-WorkdirEvidence <literal> | -NoWorkdirEvidence) [-Json]
15: #
16: #   Classify: -DispatchDir <path> -ReceiptPath <path> -ExpectedRound <label>
17: #             -ExpectedReceiptSha256 <hex> -Redeem <nonce> [-Json]
18: #
19: # -LAUNCH AND -POLL ARE GONE. This tool used to start a detached process
20: # itself and let a caller poll its liveness from a second, independent
21: # path to a verdict - the class of defect this cycle kept reproducing.
22: # -Prepare only builds the directory and prints the command; the caller
23: # dispatches that command itself as a harness-tracked background task, and
24: # the WRAPPER this tool writes calls -Classify as its last act and exits
25: # with its status. See
26: # docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-31-completion-coupled-dispatch.md.
27: #
28: # THE WRAPPER, in order: reserves BOTH a `claim` file and a `classification`
29: # file (create-new, so a second run of the same wrapper fails before
30: # touching anything) as its first act; re-verifies the working directory
31: # against the same mirror-identity values -Prepare recorded, BEFORE the
32: # lane body runs; relocates to it terminatingly; runs body.ps1 as a CHILD
33: # process (never inline, so a body that calls [Environment]::Exit cannot
34: # skip the epilogue) with its streams redirected to body.out/body.err, never
35: # to the wrapper's own stdout; re-verifies the working directory a SECOND
36: # time, after the body returns; consumes the classification reservation
37: # into `classifying:<nonce>` with a nonce minted at run time; writes the
38: # `exit` file; calls -Classify as its last act; and exits with -Classify's
39: # own exit code, so the harness task's exit code IS the classification.
40: #
41: # PARALLAX_DISPATCH_HOLD_AFTER_EXIT_WRITE, set to a path by a parent
42: # process, is BUILDER CONTRACT the wrapper honors, the same shape as the
43: # seams in tools/new-kimi-lane-home.ps1: it makes the wrapper create
44: # `<value>.started` right after the `exit` file is written, then wait,
45: # bounded at sixty seconds, for `<value>.release` before calling -Classify,
46: # failing through to a non-zero exit on timeout. Its only reachable effect
47: # is to DELAY or FAIL a wrapper - never to turn a failing round into a
48: # successful one. No shipped caller sets it.
49: #
50: # -Classify REDEEMS a reservation the wrapper already made in the
51: # `classification` file. It never creates one. It accepts exactly one
52: # content: `classifying:<nonce>` where nonce equals -Redeem; anything
53: # else (including a plain `reserved`, meaning the wrapper has not
54: # finished) is refused rather than treated as permission to proceed.
55: # Once redeemed, the resolved state name is WRITTEN into `classification`,
56: # so the file is both the reservation and the record, and every later
57: # call sees a value that is neither `reserved` nor a matching
58: # `classifying:<n>` and answers `already-classified`.
59: #
60: # In this fixed order, stopping at the first match:
61: #   1. classification absent -> never-reserved
62: #   2. classification holds 'reserved' -> not-ready
63: #   3. classification holds classifying:<n> with n not -Redeem, or
64: #      anything else -> already-classified
65: #   4. receipt absent, unreadable, or failing the schema -> no-receipt
66: #   5. receipt's dispatchDir or round is not the pair supplied
67: #      independently -> receipt-not-expected
68: #   6. the receipt's own bytes do not hash to -ExpectedReceiptSha256 ->
69: #      receipt-altered
70: #   7. no claim file in the dispatch directory -> no-claim
71: #   8. workingDirectory missing, unresolvable, or not a filesystem
72: #      container -> cwd-unreadable
73: #   9. workdirEvidence is not 'none' and no transcript file exists ->
74: #      no-transcript
75: #  10. workdirEvidence is not 'none' and the transcript's FIRST
76: #      'workdir:' header line is absent -> workdir-unconfirmed
77: #  11. that header line's value differs from workdirEvidence ->
78: #      workdir-mismatch
79: #  12. no exit file -> no-exit-file
80: #  13. exit unreadable or not a plain integer -> exit-unreadable
81: #  14. exit non-zero -> exit-nonzero
82: #  15. no reply file -> no-reply
83: #  16. reply is empty -> reply-empty
84: #  17. otherwise -> reply-present
85: #
86: # -Classify exit codes: 0 is reply-present and nothing else; 2 is a
87: # parameter-binding failure, an unrecognized argument, or an internal
88: # execution error; 1 is every other state, with the state name on stdout.
89: #
90: # -Prepare, in order, under $ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop':
91: #   1. Resolve -ReceiptPath, -DispatchDir, -WorkingDirectory. BLOCK, before
92: #      anything is created, if the receipt path is equal to or inside the
93: #      dispatch directory, if the receipt path already exists, if the
94: #      dispatch directory already exists, or if the working directory is
95: #      not an existing filesystem container.
96: #   1a. BLOCK if the working directory does not verify as the named
97: #      mirror: tools/new-review-mirror.ps1 -VerifyIdentity is run with
98: #      every value the caller supplied, and any non-zero exit BLOCKS with
99: #      a "mirror identity" message. There is no override switch - every
100: #      call site already uses the mirror.
101: #   2. Resolve -DispatchHost to a full path with Get-Command. Only 'pwsh'
102: #      and 'powershell' are accepted; anything else, or a name that does
103: #      not resolve, exits 2.
104: #   3. Read -PriorStateFile as raw bytes and hash it (SHA256).
105: #   4. Reserve the dispatch directory with New-Item -ItemType Directory
106: #      and NO -Force: a taken directory fails loudly instead of
107: #      proceeding with an unreliable path.
108: #   5. Copy -WrapperBody into the directory as body.ps1, byte for byte,
109: #      and write wrapper.ps1 beside it - the full shape described above,
110: #      written ENTIRELY by this tool and never containing the body's text.
111: #      The receipt's bytes are computed here too (in memory only) so their
112: #      digest can be embedded in the wrapper as -ExpectedReceiptSha256.
113: #   6. Write the receipt LAST, with create-new semantics, from the same
114: #      bytes just hashed. Its fields are exactly: dispatchDir, token,
115: #      round, workingDirectory, dispatchHost, priorStateSha256,
116: #      workdirEvidence, repoRoot, sourceHead, mirrorHead,
117: #      sourceStatusSha256, mirrorStateSha256, expectedMirrorPath, schema
118: #      (the integer 2).
119: #   7. Print command, taskName, wrapper, dispatchDir, round.
120: #
121: # A failure at any step leaves the reserved directory in place and no
122: # receipt. That is the shape of a handled failure, not evidence of one.
123: #
124: # Exit codes: 0 prepared, 1 blocked (reason on stdout), 2 a failure to
125: # bind or validate the parameters (an unknown mode, a missing required
126: # value, an invalid -DispatchHost) or an internal execution error.
127: #
128: # Windows PowerShell 5.1 compatible, ASCII only.
129: 
130: param(
131:     [switch]$Prepare,
132:     [string]$DispatchDir,
133:     [string]$WrapperBody,
134:     [string]$ReceiptPath,
135:     [string]$Round,
136:     [string]$WorkingDirectory,
137:     [string]$RepoRoot,
138:     [string]$SourceHead,
139:     [string]$MirrorHead,
140:     [string]$SourceStatusSha256,
141:     [string]$MirrorStateSha256,
142:     [string]$ExpectedMirrorPath,
143:     [string]$DispatchHost,
144:     [string]$PriorStateFile,
145:     [string]$WorkdirEvidence,

153:     [switch]$Json,

155:     # Catches every remaining token so an unrecognized argument is
156:     # refused rather than silently absorbed. Measured on both hosts: a
157:     # -File invocation hands this array one PHANTOM $null element even
158:     # when nothing else would land there - see tools/new-review-mirror.ps1's
159:     # own $BuildRemainingArgs handling, which this follows. Neither a
160:     # real flag name nor a real value is ever null or empty, so filtering

168: function Resolve-UnresolvedPath([string]$Path) {
169:     return $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($Path)
170: }
171: 
172: function Get-Sha256Hex([byte[]]$Bytes) {
173:     $sha = [System.Security.Cryptography.SHA256]::Create()
174:     try {
175:         $hash = $sha.ComputeHash($Bytes)
176:     } finally {
177:         $sha.Dispose()
178:     }
179:     $sb = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder
180:     foreach ($b in $hash) { [void]$sb.Append($b.ToString("x2")) }
181:     return $sb.ToString()
182: }
183: 
184: # Validates a parsed receipt against Task 2's schema: exactly the
185: # fourteen named fields, no missing field, no extra field, every string
186: # field non-null and non-empty, and `schema` a JSON number equal to 2.
187: # Any other deviation - wrong top-level type, a missing field, an empty
188: # string field, a wrong JSON type, an unknown extra field - fails it the
189: # same way; nothing branches differently on which deviation it was.
190: function Test-ReceiptSchema($Obj) {
191:     if ($null -eq $Obj) { return $false }
192:     if (-not ($Obj -is [System.Management.Automation.PSCustomObject])) {
193:         return $false
194:     }
195: 
196:     $requiredStringFields = @(
197:         'dispatchDir', 'token', 'round', 'workingDirectory', 'dispatchHost',
198:         'priorStateSha256', 'workdirEvidence', 'repoRoot', 'sourceHead',
199:         'mirrorHead', 'sourceStatusSha256', 'mirrorStateSha256',
200:         'expectedMirrorPath')
201:     $allFields = $requiredStringFields + @('schema')
202: 
203:     $propSet = New-Object 'System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]'
204:     foreach ($p in @($Obj.PSObject.Properties.Name)) { [void]$propSet.Add($p) }
205:     foreach ($f in $allFields) {
206:         if (-not $propSet.Contains($f)) { return $false }
207:     }
208:     if ($propSet.Count -ne $allFields.Count) { return $false }
209: 
210:     foreach ($f in $requiredStringFields) {
211:         $v = $Obj.$f
212:         if ($null -eq $v) { return $false }
213:         if (-not ($v -is [string])) { return $false }
214:         if ($v.Length -eq 0) { return $false }
215:     }
216: 
217:     $schemaVal = $Obj.schema
218:     if ($null -eq $schemaVal) { return $false }
219:     if ($schemaVal -is [string]) { return $false }
220:     $isNumeric = ($schemaVal -is [int]) -or ($schemaVal -is [long]) -or
221:                  ($schemaVal -is [double]) -or ($schemaVal -is [decimal])
222:     if (-not $isNumeric) { return $false }
223:     if ([double]$schemaVal -ne 2) { return $false }
224: 
225:     return $true
226: }
227: 
228: # Doubles an embedded single quote so a value can be dropped inside a
229: # single-quoted literal in generated PowerShell text without breaking it
230: # out of the quotes it is placed in.
231: function ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner([string]$Value) {
232:     return $Value.Replace("'", "''")
233: }
234: 
235: # Builds the wrapper's ENTIRE text, exactly as in the design section of
236: # docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-31-completion-coupled-dispatch.md: the
237: # claim and classification reservations, the mirror re-verify before the
238: # body runs, the body run as a CHILD process, the mirror re-verify after
239: # the body returns, the classification-reservation consume, the exit-file
240: # write, the PARALLAX_DISPATCH_HOLD_AFTER_EXIT_WRITE seam, the -Classify
241: # call, and `exit $LASTEXITCODE` as the last statement. Every interpolated
242: # value is dropped inside the template's own single quotes, with any
243: # embedded single quote doubled, so a path containing one cannot break the
244: # generated script.
245: function Get-WrapperTemplate {
246:     param(
247:         [string]$MirrorToolPath,
248:         [string]$RepoRoot,
249:         [string]$WorkingDirectory,
250:         [string]$SourceHead,
251:         [string]$MirrorHead,
252:         [string]$SourceStatusSha256,
253:         [string]$MirrorStateSha256,
254:         [string]$ExpectedMirrorPath,
255:         [string]$HostPath,
256:         [string]$ToolPath,
257:         [string]$DispatchDir,
258:         [string]$ReceiptPath,
259:         [string]$Round,
260:         [string]$ReceiptSha256
261:     )
262: 
263:     $template = @'
264: $ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
265: $utf8 = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
266: # ONE first act, two reservations, in this order. BOTH are create-new:
267: # WriteAllText is NOT create-new, it overwrites, so the reservation is
268: # opened with FileMode CreateNew and written through that handle.
269: [System.IO.File]::Open("$PSScriptRoot/claim", 'CreateNew', 'Write', 'None').Close()
270: $r = [System.IO.File]::Open("$PSScriptRoot/classification", 'CreateNew', 'Write', 'None')
271: try { $b = $utf8.GetBytes('reserved'); $r.Write($b, 0, $b.Length) } finally { $r.Close() }
272: 
273: # ONE argument set, defined once, used by BOTH verifications.
274: $mirrorArgs = @{
275:     VerifyIdentity      = $true
276:     RepoRoot            = '<repoRoot>'
277:     MirrorPath          = '<workingDirectory>'
278:     SourceHead          = '<sourceHead>'
279:     MirrorHead          = '<mirrorHead>'
280:     SourceStatusSha256  = '<sourceStatusSha256>'
281:     MirrorStateSha256   = '<mirrorStateSha256>'
282:     ExpectedMirrorPath  = '<expectedMirrorPath>'
283: }
284: 
285: # The tree is verified HERE, at run time, not merely at preparation.
286: & '<mirrorToolPath>' @mirrorArgs > "$PSScriptRoot/mirror.verify" 2>&1
287: if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'mirror identity failed at dispatch time' }
288: 
289: Set-Location -LiteralPath '<workingDirectory>' -ErrorAction Stop
290: $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
291: $null | & '<hostPath>' -NoProfile -NonInteractive -File "$PSScriptRoot/body.ps1" `
292:     > "$PSScriptRoot/body.out" 2> "$PSScriptRoot/body.err"
293: $code = $LASTEXITCODE
294: # NOT a complete backstop, and the comment must not imply it is. If the
295: # resolved host binary has been deleted since preparation, the call raises
296: # a statement-terminating error under Continue and $LASTEXITCODE still
297: # holds the FIRST verification's zero - a stale 0, not $null, so this line
298: # does not fire. The round still cannot read as success: the body never
299: # ran, so there is no reply and no transcript, and classification lands on
300: # no-transcript or no-reply. But the exit file then records a 0 for a
301: # client that never launched. Conservative, and worth knowing when reading
302: # that file.
303: if ($null -eq $code) { $code = 1 }
304: 
305: # The tree is verified a SECOND time, after the client has finished, so a
306: # mutation that persisted through the round is caught. A change reverted
307: # inside the round is not, and no before-and-after check could catch it.
308: # 'Stop' is restored FIRST: see the note below, this line is load-bearing.
309: $ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
310: & '<mirrorToolPath>' @mirrorArgs >> "$PSScriptRoot/mirror.verify" 2>&1
311: if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'the mirror changed while the round ran' }
312: 
313: # CONSUME the reservation BEFORE any terminal artifact is published, and
314: # stamp it with a nonce minted HERE, at run time, that appears in no file
315: # -Prepare wrote.
316: $nonce = [System.Guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N')
317: [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText("$PSScriptRoot/classification", "classifying:$nonce", $utf8)
318: [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText("$PSScriptRoot/exit", "$code", $utf8)
319: # test seam: PARALLAX_DISPATCH_HOLD_AFTER_EXIT_WRITE, see below. BUILDER
320: # CONTRACT, the same shape as the seams in tools/new-kimi-lane-home.ps1:
321: # any parent process can set it, no shipped caller does, and its only
322: # reachable effect is to DELAY or FAIL this wrapper - never to turn a
323: # failing round into a successful one.
324: $holdPath = $env:PARALLAX_DISPATCH_HOLD_AFTER_EXIT_WRITE
325: if ($holdPath) {
326:     [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText("$holdPath.started", 'started', $utf8)
327:     $deadline = (Get-Date).AddSeconds(60)
328:     while (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath "$holdPath.release")) {
329:         if ((Get-Date) -ge $deadline) {
330:             exit 1
331:         }
332:         Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200
333:     }
334: }
335: & '<toolPath>' -Classify -DispatchDir '<dispatchDir>' -ReceiptPath '<receiptPath>' `
336:     -ExpectedRound '<round>' -ExpectedReceiptSha256 '<receiptSha256>' `
337:     -Redeem $nonce
338: exit $LASTEXITCODE
339: '@
340: 
341:     $template = $template.Replace('<repoRoot>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $RepoRoot))
342:     $template = $template.Replace('<workingDirectory>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $WorkingDirectory))
343:     $template = $template.Replace('<sourceHead>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $SourceHead))
344:     $template = $template.Replace('<mirrorHead>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $MirrorHead))
345:     $template = $template.Replace('<sourceStatusSha256>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $SourceStatusSha256))
346:     $template = $template.Replace('<mirrorStateSha256>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $MirrorStateSha256))
347:     $template = $template.Replace('<expectedMirrorPath>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $ExpectedMirrorPath))
348:     $template = $template.Replace('<mirrorToolPath>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $MirrorToolPath))
349:     $template = $template.Replace('<hostPath>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $HostPath))
350:     $template = $template.Replace('<toolPath>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $ToolPath))
351:     $template = $template.Replace('<dispatchDir>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $DispatchDir))
352:     $template = $template.Replace('<receiptPath>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $ReceiptPath))
353:     $template = $template.Replace('<round>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $Round))
354:     $template = $template.Replace('<receiptSha256>', (ConvertTo-PSSingleQuoteInner $ReceiptSha256))
355: 
356:     return $template -replace '\r?\n', "`r`n"
357: }
358: 
359: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
360: # Reject unknown arguments for BOTH modes, before anything else runs.
361: # See the $Rest declaration above for why the phantom filter is needed.
362: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
363: $realRest = @($Rest | Where-Object { $_ })
364: if ($realRest.Count -gt 0) {
365:     Write-Output ("ERROR: unrecognized parameter '" + [string]$realRest[0] + "'")
366:     exit 2
367: }
368: 
369: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
370: # Mode selection and required-value checks are done by hand: every
371: # parameter above is a plain [string] or [switch], never PowerShell's own
372: # Mandatory attribute, so a caller who names the wrong mode (including an
373: # old -Launch or -Poll, which no longer bind to anything) falls through to
374: # this explicit check rather than into the binder's own error shape.
375: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
376: if ((-not $Prepare) -and (-not $Classify)) {
377:     Write-Output "ERROR: specify -Prepare or -Classify"
378:     exit 2
379: }
380: if ($Prepare -and $Classify) {
381:     Write-Output "ERROR: specify exactly one of -Prepare or -Classify"
382:     exit 2
383: }
384: 
385: if ($Prepare) {
386: 
387: $requiredValues = [ordered]@{
388:     DispatchDir        = $DispatchDir
389:     WrapperBody        = $WrapperBody
390:     ReceiptPath        = $ReceiptPath
391:     Round              = $Round
392:     WorkingDirectory   = $WorkingDirectory
393:     RepoRoot           = $RepoRoot
394:     SourceHead         = $SourceHead
395:     MirrorHead         = $MirrorHead
396:     SourceStatusSha256 = $SourceStatusSha256
397:     MirrorStateSha256  = $MirrorStateSha256
398:     ExpectedMirrorPath = $ExpectedMirrorPath
399:     DispatchHost       = $DispatchHost
400:     PriorStateFile     = $PriorStateFile

616: # the dispatch directory is in. See the header for the full state order.
617: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------

619:     DispatchDir           = $DispatchDir

629:     }

635: } catch {

637:     exit 2

639: 
640: function Write-ClassifyState([string]$State, $RoundOut, [bool]$RecordFile) {
641:     if ($RecordFile) {
642:         $classificationPath = Join-Path $dispatchFull "classification"
643:         $utf8 = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
644:         [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($classificationPath, $State, $utf8)
645:     }
646:     if ($Json) {
647:         $obj = [ordered]@{ state = $State; round = $RoundOut }
648:         Write-Output (ConvertTo-Json $obj -Compress)
649:     } else {
650:         Write-Output $State
651:     }
652: }
653: 
654: $classificationPath = Join-Path $dispatchFull "classification"
655: 
656: # 1. classification absent -> never-reserved
657: if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $classificationPath -PathType Leaf)) {
658:     Write-ClassifyState "never-reserved" $null $false
659:     exit 1
660: }
661: 
662: try {
663:     $classContent = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($classificationPath, [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8)
664: } catch {
665:     Write-Output ("ERROR: could not read classification: " + $_.Exception.Message)
666:     exit 2
667: }
668: 
669: # 2. classification holds 'reserved' -> not-ready
670: if ($classContent -ceq 'reserved') {
671:     Write-ClassifyState "not-ready" $null $false
672:     exit 1
673: }
674: 
675: # 3. classifying:<n> with n != -Redeem, or anything else -> already-classified
676: $expectedReservation = "classifying:" + $Redeem
677: if ($classContent -cne $expectedReservation) {
678:     Write-ClassifyState "already-classified" $null $false
679:     exit 1
680: }
681: 
682: # From here, this call owns the reservation: the resolved state is
683: # WRITTEN into `classification`, so the file is both the reservation and
684: # the record.
685: $roundOut = $null
686: 
687: # 4. receipt absent, unreadable, or failing the schema -> no-receipt
688: if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $receiptFull -PathType Leaf)) {
689:     Write-ClassifyState "no-receipt" $null $true
690:     exit 1
691: }
692: $receiptBytes = $null
693: $receiptObj = $null
694: try {
695:     $receiptBytes = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($receiptFull)
696:     $receiptText = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetString($receiptBytes)
697:     $receiptObj = $receiptText | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop
698: } catch {
699:     $receiptObj = $null
700: }
701: if (-not (Test-ReceiptSchema $receiptObj)) {
702:     Write-ClassifyState "no-receipt" $null $true
703:     exit 1
704: }
705: $roundOut = [string]$receiptObj.round
706: 
707: # 5. receipt's dispatchDir or round is not the pair supplied
708: #    independently -> receipt-not-expected
709: $receiptDispatchDir = [string]$receiptObj.dispatchDir
710: $dNormC = $dispatchFull.TrimEnd('\', '/')
711: $rdNormC = $receiptDispatchDir.TrimEnd('\', '/')
712: $cmpC = [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
713: if ((-not [string]::Equals($rdNormC, $dNormC, $cmpC)) -or
714:         ($receiptObj.round -cne $ExpectedRound)) {
715:     Write-ClassifyState "receipt-not-expected" $roundOut $true
716:     exit 1
717: }
718: 
719: # 6. the receipt's own bytes do not hash to the digest the wrapper
720: #    carries -> receipt-altered
721: $actualSha = Get-Sha256Hex $receiptBytes
722: if ($actualSha.ToLowerInvariant() -ne $ExpectedReceiptSha256.ToLowerInvariant()) {
723:     Write-ClassifyState "receipt-altered" $roundOut $true
724:     exit 1
725: }
726: 
727: # 7. no claim file in the dispatch directory -> no-claim
728: if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dispatchFull "claim") -PathType Leaf)) {
729:     Write-ClassifyState "no-claim" $roundOut $true
730:     exit 1
731: }
732: 
733: # 8. workingDirectory missing, unresolvable, or not a filesystem
734: #    container -> cwd-unreadable
735: $workingDirFull = $null
736: try {
737:     $workingDirFull = Resolve-UnresolvedPath ([string]$receiptObj.workingDirectory)
738: } catch {
739:     $workingDirFull = $null
740: }
741: if ((-not $workingDirFull) -or
742:         (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $workingDirFull -PathType Container))) {
743:     Write-ClassifyState "cwd-unreadable" $roundOut $true
744:     exit 1
745: }
746: 
747: # 9-11. the working-directory evidence check, skipped only on the exact
748: # literal 'none'. The header is PARSED - the FIRST 'workdir:' line only -
749: # never searched for, because the transcript is prompt-steerable.
750: $workdirEvidence = [string]$receiptObj.workdirEvidence
751: if ($workdirEvidence -cne 'none') {
752:     $transcriptPath = Join-Path $dispatchFull "transcript"
753:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $transcriptPath -PathType Leaf)) {
754:         Write-ClassifyState "no-transcript" $roundOut $true
755:         exit 1
756:     }
757:     $transcriptLines = @(Get-Content -LiteralPath $transcriptPath)
758:     $headerValue = $null
759:     foreach ($line in $transcriptLines) {
760:         $lineStr = [string]$line
761:         if ($lineStr.StartsWith("workdir:")) {
762:             $headerValue = $lineStr.Substring(8).Trim()
763:             break
764:         }
765:     }
766:     if ($null -eq $headerValue) {
767:         Write-ClassifyState "workdir-unconfirmed" $roundOut $true
768:         exit 1
769:     }
770:     if ($headerValue -cne $workdirEvidence) {
771:         Write-ClassifyState "workdir-mismatch" $roundOut $true
772:         exit 1
773:     }
774: }
775: 
776: # 12. no exit file -> no-exit-file
777: $exitPath = Join-Path $dispatchFull "exit"
778: if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $exitPath -PathType Leaf)) {
779:     Write-ClassifyState "no-exit-file" $roundOut $true
780:     exit 1
781: }
782: 
783: # 13. exit unreadable or not a plain integer -> exit-unreadable
784: $exitContent = $null
785: try {
786:     $exitContent = ([System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($exitPath)).Trim()
787: } catch {
788:     $exitContent = $null
789: }
790: if (($null -eq $exitContent) -or ($exitContent -notmatch '^-?\d+$')) {
791:     Write-ClassifyState "exit-unreadable" $roundOut $true
792:     exit 1
793: }
794: $exitCode = [int]$exitContent
795: 
796: # 14. exit non-zero -> exit-nonzero
797: if ($exitCode -ne 0) {
798:     Write-ClassifyState "exit-nonzero" $roundOut $true
799:     exit 1
800: }
801: 
802: # 15. no reply file -> no-reply
803: $replyPath = Join-Path $dispatchFull "reply"
804: if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $replyPath -PathType Leaf)) {
805:     Write-ClassifyState "no-reply" $roundOut $true
806:     exit 1
807: }
808: 
809: # 16. reply is empty -> reply-empty
810: if ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $replyPath).Length -eq 0) {
811:     Write-ClassifyState "reply-empty" $roundOut $true
812:     exit 1
813: }
814: 
815: # 17. otherwise -> reply-present
816: Write-ClassifyState "reply-present" $roundOut $true
817: exit 0
818: 
819: }

27: # WINDOWS ONLY, for the same reason as the probe's suite: the mirror runs
28: # the probe, which resolves the reviewer's global instruction file under
29: # `$env:USERPROFILE`, and the lane lock lives under `$env:LOCALAPPDATA`.
30: # Neither exists on Linux, so the run refuses to report a measurement it
31: # could not make - the script working, not failing.
32: #
33: # Guarding only on a PowerShell host being present was not enough: the CI
34: # runner ships `pwsh`, so these cases ran on Linux and failed there for a
35: # second, different reason. See the probe suite's header for the full
36: # sequence.
37: #
38: # COVERAGE, recorded rather than assumed: these cases are Windows-only, so
39: # the ubuntu job skips them. The `powershell-hosts` job on windows-latest
40: # runs this module under BOTH powershell.exe and pwsh.exe. If this module
41: # ever leaves both Windows pytest steps, that claim is false and
42: # test_backup_lane.py asserts it back.
43: POWERSHELL = (os.environ.get("PARALLAX_PS_HOST")
44:               or shutil.which("powershell") or shutil.which("pwsh"))
45: 
46: pytestmark = pytest.mark.skipif(
47:     os.name != "nt" or POWERSHELL is None,
48:     reason="the review mirror is a Windows tool: it needs a PowerShell "
49:            "host and the Windows profile variables it measures")

51: 
52: def ps_host():
53:     return POWERSHELL

56: def git(repo, *args):
57:     return subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(repo), *args],
58:                           capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout
59: 
60: 
61: def commit(repo, message):
62:     git(repo, "-c", "user.email=t@t", "-c", "user.name=t",
63:         "commit", "-q", "-m", message)
64: 
65: 
66: def make_repo(tmp_path):
67:     """A scratch repo with one tracked file, one ignored file, and one
68:     untracked file, so the baseline has something to carry."""
69:     repo = tmp_path / "src"
70:     repo.mkdir()
71:     git(tmp_path, "init", "-q", str(repo))
72:     (repo / "kept.txt").write_text("tracked\n")
73:     (repo / ".gitignore").write_text("ignored/\n")
74:     (repo / "ignored").mkdir()
75:     (repo / "ignored" / "secret.txt").write_text("gitignored input\n")
76:     (repo / "untracked.txt").write_text("untracked\n")
77:     git(repo, "add", "kept.txt", ".gitignore")
78:     commit(repo, "base")
79:     return repo
80: 
81: 
82: def make_clean_repo(tmp_path):
83:     """A repo with NOTHING for the baseline to carry: no back-channels, no
84:     untracked files, no ignored files. The ordinary case, and the one an
85:     empty-array return would have misread as an enumeration failure."""
86:     repo = tmp_path / "clean"
87:     repo.mkdir()
88:     git(tmp_path, "init", "-q", str(repo))
89:     (repo / "only.txt").write_text("tracked\n")
90:     git(repo, "add", "only.txt")
91:     commit(repo, "base")
92:     return repo
93: 
94: 
95: def run_mirror(repo, mirror, *extra, env=None):
96:     """`env` carries the build's three TEST SEAMS.
97: 
98:     They are environment variables, not parameters, for the reason the
99:     lane-home builder states about its own two: a parameter is public
100:     surface on a shipped tool. No shipped caller sets either.
101: 
102:     Each is a BOOLEAN that ORs one extra block condition into a
103:     comparison. It cannot supply a value, so it cannot suppress a real
104:     measurement, and setting it can only ever ADD a reason to fail.
105:     That is what makes the one-way claim structural rather than a
106:     property of the value someone happens to pass.
107: 
108:     Two earlier shapes failed that bar and the whole-branch review
109:     caught both. The first copied from a different tree and committed
110:     into the real source repo. The second INJECTED a value, so a parent
111:     that set the variable to the repo's own current HEAD - trivially
112:     readable in advance - suppressed the genuine post-copy measurement
113:     and turned a build that should have failed into one that passed.
114:     """
115:     full = None
116:     if env:
117:         full = dict(os.environ)
118:         full.update(env)
119:     return subprocess.run(
120:         [ps_host(), "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-File", str(MIRROR),
121:          "-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-MirrorPath", str(mirror),
122:          "-SkipProbe", *extra],
123:         capture_output=True, text=True, env=full)
124: 
125: 
126: SKIP_BLOCK = "BLOCKED: no client measurement was made (-SkipProbe)"
127: 
128: 
129: def assert_built(proc):
130:     """-SkipProbe builds the mirror but VERIFIES nothing, so it exits 1.
131: 
132:     The exit code reports dispatch-readiness, not construction: a mirror
133:     with no client measurement is not cleared for dispatch, and must not
134:     share an exit code with one that is. The mode-diff review of
135:     2026-07-28 found the old exit 0 there.
136: 
137:     Asserting the SPECIFIC skip line is what keeps these tests able to
138:     catch a real construction failure, which also exits 1 but with a
139:     different reason.
140:     """
141:     assert proc.returncode == 1, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
142:     assert SKIP_BLOCK in proc.stdout, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
143: 
144: 
145: def make_junction(link, target):
146:     """A junction via mklink /J, or skip. Junctions need no privilege;
147:     symbolic links do, and this session cannot make one."""
148:     rc = subprocess.run(["cmd", "/c", "mklink", "/J", str(link), str(target)],
149:                         capture_output=True, text=True)
150:     if rc.returncode != 0:
151:         pytest.skip("junction creation unavailable: " + rc.stderr)
152: 
153: 
154: def unlink_junction(link):
155:     """rmdir removes the junction and never its target."""
156:     subprocess.run(["cmd", "/c", "rmdir", str(link)],
157:                    capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
158: 
159: 
160: def is_reparse_point(path):
161:     """Python's is_symlink() is False for a junction, so read the
162:     attribute bit the filesystem sets for both kinds of link."""
163:     return bool(os.lstat(str(path)).st_file_attributes
164:                 & stat.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT)
165: 
166: 
167: def read_block(stdout, label):
168:     """Lines of one labelled block from the record output."""
169:     lines = stdout.splitlines()
170:     start = next(i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if line.startswith(label))
171:     out = []
172:     for line in lines[start + 1:]:
173:         if line and not line.startswith("  "):
174:             break
175:         if line.strip():
176:             out.append(line.strip())
177:     return out
178: 
179: 
638: def run_functions(snippet):
639:     """Dot-source the mirror script's function block, then run `snippet`.
640: 
641:     Same slicing the probe tests use. It exists here because git quotes a
642:     pathname containing a double quote, a backslash or a control character
643:     whatever `core.quotepath` says - and Windows permits none of those in a
644:     filename, so the guard for that residue cannot be reached by building a
645:     real repo.
646: 
647:     The block goes through a FILE, not `-Command`, for the same reason the
648:     probe's copy does: passed inline it outgrows the Windows command-line
649:     limit as the script gains rules, and the failure then looks like the
650:     code under test rather than like the harness. The BOM is what makes
651:     Windows PowerShell 5.1 read the file as UTF-8.
652:     """
653:     text = MIRROR.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
654:     body = text[text.index(BODY_START):text.index(BODY_END)]
655:     with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile("w", suffix=".ps1", delete=False,
656:                                      encoding="utf-8-sig") as fh:
657:         # Stop on the FIRST error. Measured 2026-07-29 on both hosts: an
658:         # undefined function is NON-terminating here, the host exits 0, and
659:         # the snippet's partial output comes back - so a missing or
660:         # mistyped function reads as a wrong VALUE instead of an error.
661:         # These snippets exercise pathname handling; a quiet failure is the
662:         # one outcome this module must not produce.
663:         fh.write('$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"\n' + body + "\n" + snippet)
664:         path = fh.name
665:     try:
666:         proc = subprocess.run(
667:             [ps_host(), "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-File", path],
668:             capture_output=True, text=True)
669:     finally:
670:         os.unlink(path)
671:     assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
672:     return proc.stdout.strip()
673:

675: def test_an_empty_capture_is_a_legitimate_state():
676:     # A repo with nothing to list produces no bytes. That is not a failure,
677:     # and the wrapper is what keeps it from reading as one.
678:     out = run_functions(
679:         '$r = ConvertFrom-NulCapture ([byte[]]@())\n'
680:         '"{0}|{1}" -f $r.Ok, (@($r.Fields).Count)')
681:     assert out == "True|0", out

684: def test_fields_split_on_nul_and_keep_their_spaces():
685:     # THE WHOLE POINT. A space in a pathname is what made git quote it, and
686:     # under -z the space is simply part of the field.
687:     out = run_functions(
688:         '$b = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes("M+ Timer/a.lua`0b.lua`0")\n'
689:         '$r = ConvertFrom-NulCapture $b\n'
690:         '(@($r.Fields) -join "|")')
691:     assert out == "M+ Timer/a.lua|b.lua", out

696:     # fragment into the manifest under the name of a real file.
697:     out = run_functions(

699:         '$r = ConvertFrom-NulCapture $b\n'
700:         '"{0}|{1}" -f $r.Ok, $r.Reason')
701:     assert out.startswith("False|"), out
702:     assert "does not end with a NUL" in out, out

705: def test_invalid_utf8_stops_instead_of_being_replaced():
706:     # The reason the bytes are read raw. PowerShell's own decode maps a
707:     # malformed byte to U+FFFD silently, which on this boundary is a wrong
708:     # pathname reported as a good one.
709:     out = run_functions(
710:         '$b = [byte[]]@(97, 255, 0)\n'
711:         '$r = ConvertFrom-NulCapture $b\n'
712:         '"{0}|{1}" -f $r.Ok, $r.Reason')
713:     assert out.startswith("False|"), out
714:     assert "not valid UTF-8" in out, out

717: def test_a_non_ascii_field_arrives_as_one_character_not_two_bytes():
718:     # THE DECODER DEFECT THIS CYCLE DELETES, pinned so it cannot return.
719:     # The old ConvertFrom-GitQuotedPath turned the ESCAPE PAIR
720:     # `\303\251` into character codes 195,169 - two characters -
721:     # where the byte pair names one, code 233. (The surrounding name
722:     # decoded normally: `caf\303\251` came out five characters, not
723:     # two. The two here are the escapes' own output.) Reading raw bytes
724:     # and decoding once from UTF-8 is what makes that impossible.
725:     out = run_functions(
726:         '$b = [byte[]]@(99, 97, 102, 195, 169, 0)\n'
727:         '$r = ConvertFrom-NulCapture $b\n'
728:         '(([int[]][char[]]$r.Fields[0]) -join ",")')
729:     assert out == "99,97,102,233", out
730:

732: def test_an_empty_field_before_the_end_is_kept_for_the_parser_to_refuse():
733:     # Only the empty string AFTER the final NUL is dropped. An empty field
734:     # anywhere else is a real fault and must reach the caller rather than
735:     # being tidied away here.
736:     out = run_functions(
737:         '$b = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes("a.txt`0`0b.txt`0")\n'
738:         '$r = ConvertFrom-NulCapture $b\n'

740:     assert out == "3|a.txt,,b.txt", out

743: def test_the_supported_pathname_guard_is_pinned_in_both_directions():
744:     # The guard admits exactly what Format-StatusPathname can record
745:     # exactly: an ordinary name, and a name whose ONLY quoting trigger is a
746:     # space. Everything the porcelain line form would quote or escape some
747:     # other way is refused, plus `>` for the arrow separator. Pinned in
748:     # both directions so a future edit cannot make the guard constant.
749:     out = run_functions(

755:         '  (Test-SupportedPathname "a$([char]10)b.txt"),\n'

757:         '  (Test-SupportedPathname ("a" + $q + "b.txt")),\n'
758:         '  (Test-SupportedPathname "a\\b.txt"),\n'
759:         '  (Test-SupportedPathname "a>b.txt"),\n'

764:                    "False,False,False,False"), out

767: def test_a_rename_record_reads_destination_first_then_source():
1225: def long_mirror(tmp_path):
1226:     """A mirror root deliberately much LONGER than the source repo root.
1227: 
1228:     The source has to be creatable or the fixture measures Python's
1229:     limits instead of the tool's: a destination at 280 characters built
1230:     from a source root the same length needs a 280-character source,
1231:     which `mkdir` refuses before the tool ever runs. `make_repo` uses
1232:     `src`, so this name buys 37 characters of headroom.
1233:     """
1234:     return tmp_path / ("mirrorroot" * 4)
1235: 
1236: 
1237: def deep_child(root, total_len, tail="f.txt"):
1238:     """A path under `root` whose FULL length is exactly `total_len`,
1239:     built from nested directories so no single component is
1240:     unreasonable."""
1241:     room = total_len - len(str(root)) - 1 - len(tail)
1242:     assert room >= 2, "root already too long for this case"
1243:     parts = []
1244:     while room > 31:
1245:         parts.append("d" * 29)
1246:         room -= 30
1247:     parts.append("d" * (room - 1))
1248:     return root.joinpath(*parts) / tail
1249: 
1250: 
1251: def budget_error(proc):
1252:     """The BUDGET refusal line, or None.
1253: 
1254:     Matches the full phrase, not the words "path budget": the
1255:     unenumerable-path refusal says "the path budget was never measured",
1256:     so a looser match let three cases below accept an enumeration block
1257:     as a budget refusal. Found by the Fable review of this task.
1258:     """
1259:     for line in proc.stdout.splitlines():
1260:         if "path budget exceeded" in line.lower():
1261:             return line
1262:     return None
1263: 
1264: 
1265: def test_a_mirror_just_under_the_budget_builds(tmp_path):
1266:     """The positive control. Without it every refusal below is satisfied
1267:     by a tool that refuses everything."""
1268:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1269:     mirror = long_mirror(tmp_path)
1270:     target = deep_child(mirror, BUDGET - 1)
1271:     src = repo / target.relative_to(mirror)
1272:     src.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
1273:     src.write_text("deep but legal\n")
1274:     proc = run_mirror(repo, mirror)
1275:     assert_built(proc)
1276:     assert target.exists(), "a path one under the budget must be copied"
1277: 
1278: 
1279: def test_a_mirror_at_the_budget_is_refused(tmp_path):
1280:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1281:     mirror = long_mirror(tmp_path)
1282:     src = repo / deep_child(mirror, BUDGET).relative_to(mirror)
1283:     src.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
1284:     src.write_text("exactly at the limit\n")
1285:     proc = run_mirror(repo, mirror)
1286:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1287:     assert budget_error(proc), proc.stdout
