1289: 
1290: def test_the_refusal_names_every_number_it_used(tmp_path):
1291:     """A refusal an operator cannot act on is a refusal they will
1292:     override. It must show the root length, the deepest relative path
1293:     length, their sum, and the limit."""
1294:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1295:     mirror = long_mirror(tmp_path)
1296:     deepest = deep_child(mirror, BUDGET + 20)
1297:     src = repo / deepest.relative_to(mirror)
1298:     src.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
1299:     src.write_text("over\n")
1300:     proc = run_mirror(repo, mirror)
1301:     line = budget_error(proc)
1302:     assert line, proc.stdout
1303:     assert str(len(str(mirror))) in line, ("mirror-root length missing", line)
1304:     assert str(len(str(deepest.relative_to(mirror)))) in line, (
1305:         "deepest relative path length missing", line)
1306:     assert str(BUDGET + 20) in line, ("the sum missing", line)
1307:     assert str(BUDGET) in line, ("the limit missing", line)

1310: def test_the_mirror_does_not_exist_after_a_refusal(tmp_path):
1311:     """The whole point of a PRE-flight. A refusal that leaves a directory
1312:     behind has already done part of the thing it refused to do."""
1313:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1314:     mirror = long_mirror(tmp_path)
1315:     src = repo / deep_child(mirror, BUDGET + 5).relative_to(mirror)
1316:     src.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
1317:     src.write_text("over\n")
1318:     proc = run_mirror(repo, mirror)
1319:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1320:     assert not mirror.exists(), "the pre-flight ran after creating the root"
1321: 
1322: 
1323: def test_an_over_budget_directory_with_no_files_is_refused(tmp_path):
1324:     """A DIRECTORY is a destination robocopy /E creates. Measuring only
1325:     files reads this repo as fine and then fails mid-copy."""
1326:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1327:     mirror = long_mirror(tmp_path)
1328:     d = repo / deep_child(mirror, BUDGET + 8, tail="leafdir").relative_to(mirror)
1329:     d.mkdir(parents=True)
1330:     proc = run_mirror(repo, mirror)
1331:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1332:     assert budget_error(proc), proc.stdout

1335: def test_an_over_budget_path_under_dot_git_is_refused(tmp_path):
1336:     """.git is copied, so .git counts. Skipping it is the narrower
1337:     universe the frozen sentence rules out."""
1338:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1339:     mirror = long_mirror(tmp_path)
1340:     rel = deep_child(mirror / ".git", BUDGET + 8).relative_to(mirror / ".git")
1341:     src = repo / ".git" / rel
1342:     src.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
1343:     src.write_text("git internals are destinations too\n")
1344:     proc = run_mirror(repo, mirror)
1345:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1346:     assert budget_error(proc), proc.stdout

1348: 
1349: def test_an_over_budget_override_path_is_refused(tmp_path):
1350:     """-OverrideOut is written BESIDE the mirror by this tool, not by
1351:     robocopy, so the copy universe never covers it. It gets its own
1352:     check or it gets none."""
1353:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1354:     mirror = long_mirror(tmp_path)
1355:     override = deep_child(tmp_path, BUDGET + 4, tail="o.txt")
1356:     # Deliberately NOT created. Windows caps directory creation at 248
1357:     # characters, so a fixture that built this parent would measure
1358:     # `mkdir`'s limit instead of the tool's, and the check under test
1359:     # runs on the resolved string before anything touches disk.
1360:     proc = run_mirror(repo, mirror, "-OverrideOut", str(override))
1361:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1362:     assert budget_error(proc), proc.stdout
1363:     assert not mirror.exists()

1365: 
1524: def record_field(stdout, name):
1525:     """One scalar field of the construction record, or None."""
1526:     for line in stdout.splitlines():
1527:         if line.startswith(name + ": "):
1528:             return line[len(name) + 2:].strip()
1529:     return None
1530: 
1531: 
1532: def build_and_read(repo, mirror, *extra):
1533:     proc = run_mirror(repo, mirror, *extra)
1534:     assert_built(proc)
1535:     return proc, {
1536:         "source_head": record_field(proc.stdout, "source_head"),
1537:         "mirror_head": record_field(proc.stdout, "mirror_head"),
1538:         "source_status_sha256": record_field(proc.stdout,
1539:                                              "source_status_sha256"),
1540:         "mirror_state_sha256": record_field(proc.stdout,
1541:                                             "mirror_state_sha256"),
1542:         "mirror": record_field(proc.stdout, "mirror"),
1543:     }
1544: 
1545: 
1546: def run_verify(repo, mirror, ident, **override):
1547:     """`mirror` is BOTH the live -MirrorPath argument (2nd positional)
1548:     AND, by default, -ExpectedMirrorPath - the path the identity was
1549:     recorded at. Pass `mirror=` in `override` to point the two at
1550:     different places on purpose (test_verify_refuses_a_mirror_at_a_
1551:     different_path)."""
1552:     fields = dict(ident)
1553:     fields.update(override)
1554:     return subprocess.run(
1555:         [ps_host(), "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-File", str(MIRROR),
1556:          "-VerifyIdentity", "-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-MirrorPath", str(mirror),
1557:          "-ExpectedMirrorPath", str(fields["mirror"]),
1558:          "-SourceHead", str(fields["source_head"]),
1559:          "-MirrorHead", str(fields["mirror_head"]),
1560:          "-SourceStatusSha256", str(fields["source_status_sha256"]),
1561:          "-MirrorStateSha256", str(fields["mirror_state_sha256"])],
1562:         capture_output=True, text=True)
1563: 
1564: 
1565: # --- construction: the record carries both identities ----------------
1566: 
1567: def test_the_record_carries_both_heads_and_the_source_status_hash(tmp_path):
1568:     """The positive control for construction.
1569: 
1570:     `mirror_head` differs from `source_head` whenever remediation
1571:     committed, which is the ordinary case for a repo carrying a tracked
1572:     back-channel - so the two fields are not interchangeable and a
1573:     record printing one twice would be wrong in the common case.
1574:     """
1575:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1576:     proc, ident = build_and_read(repo, tmp_path / "mirror")
1577:     assert ident["source_head"], proc.stdout
1578:     assert ident["mirror_head"], proc.stdout
1579:     assert len(ident["source_status_sha256"]) == 64, proc.stdout
1580:     assert ident["source_head"] == git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").strip()
1581: 
1582: 
1583: def test_remediation_moves_mirror_head_away_from_source_head(tmp_path):
1584:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1585:     (repo / "AGENTS.md").write_text("# planted\n")
1586:     git(repo, "add", "AGENTS.md")
1587:     commit(repo, "plant")
1588:     proc, ident = build_and_read(repo, tmp_path / "mirror")
1589:     assert ident["source_head"] != ident["mirror_head"], (
1590:         "remediation committed, so the two identities must differ; a "
1591:         "record that reported one for both would hide the commit")
1592: 
1593: 
1594: def test_a_mirror_not_built_from_this_source_blocks_at_step_four(tmp_path):
1595:     """The bridge itself.
1596: 
1597:     A pre-existing directory at the mirror path holding a DIFFERENT
1598:     repository is replaced by -Force, so this case plants the foreign
1599:     repo and passes -Force: what it checks is that step 4 compares the
1600:     COPIED tree's HEAD against the captured source HEAD rather than
1601:     trusting that the copy happened.
1602:     """
1603:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1604:     other = make_clean_repo(tmp_path)
1605:     mirror = tmp_path / "mirror"
1606:     del other
1607:     proc = run_mirror(repo, mirror,
1608:                       env={"PARALLAX_MIRROR_SEAM_FAIL_COPIED_HEAD": "1"})
1609:     assert proc.returncode == 1, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1610:     assert "was not built from" in proc.stdout.lower(), proc.stdout
1611:

1616:     proc = run_mirror(repo, mirror,
1617:                       env={"PARALLAX_MIRROR_SEAM_FAIL_SOURCE_STABLE": "1"})
1618:     assert proc.returncode == 1, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1619:     assert "moved during construction" in proc.stdout.lower(), proc.stdout

1624: def test_a_clean_dispatch_passes(tmp_path):
1625:     """The other positive control. Without it every block below is
1626:     satisfied by a gate that blocks everything."""

1628:     mirror = tmp_path / "mirror"
1629:     _, ident = build_and_read(repo, mirror)
1630:     proc = run_verify(repo, mirror, ident)
1631:     assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr

1658: def test_source_drift_in_an_ignored_file_blocks_the_dispatch(tmp_path):
1659:     """The case the two-HEAD gate CANNOT see, and the reason the source
1660:     status is captured at all.
1661: 
1662:     An edit to an ignored review input moves neither HEAD. Ignored
1663:     content is precisely what the mirror exists to carry, so leaving
1664:     this undetected would be a hole in the middle of the feature.
1665:     """

1667:     mirror = tmp_path / "mirror"

1672:     assert "source status" in proc.stdout.lower(), proc.stdout

1685: def test_a_missing_identity_field_blocks_the_dispatch(tmp_path):
1686:     """An identity that was never recorded is not an identity that
1687:     matched. Empty must never read as agreement."""
1688:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1689:     mirror = tmp_path / "mirror"
1690:     _, ident = build_and_read(repo, mirror)
1691:     for field in ("source_head", "mirror_head", "source_status_sha256",
1692:                   "mirror_state_sha256"):
1693:         proc = run_verify(repo, mirror, ident, **{field: ""})
1694:         assert proc.returncode == 1, (field, proc.stdout + proc.stderr)

1697: def test_an_unreadable_mirror_blocks_the_dispatch(tmp_path):
1698:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1699:     mirror = tmp_path / "mirror"
1700:     _, ident = build_and_read(repo, mirror)
1701:     shutil.rmtree(mirror, onerror=_force_remove)
1702:     proc = run_verify(repo, mirror, ident)
1703:     assert proc.returncode == 1, proc.stdout + proc.stderr

1706: 
1707: def test_an_unreadable_source_input_is_named_not_silently_hashed(tmp_path):
1708:     """An unmeasured file must be REPORTED, never given another file's
1709:     hash.
1710: 
1711:     Measured on both hosts 2026-08-04: a failed
1712:     `[IO.File]::ReadAllBytes` is NON-terminating, so the loop variable
1713:     keeps the PREVIOUS file's bytes and the manifest records that hash
1714:     for the file it could not read. Deterministic, so the wrong value
1715:     reproduces on every later run.
1716: 
1717:     SCOPE, stated narrowly because the Fable review's version of this
1718:     finding was wider than what is reachable: an unreadable file present
1719:     at CONSTRUCTION cannot produce a clean build, because robocopy fails
1720:     with exit 9 and the build stops before any of this. So no false-clean
1721:     identity was reachable. What was reachable, and is what this case
1722:     pins, is a verify-time read failure being reported as ordinary DRIFT
1723:     rather than as a measurement that could not be made.
1724:     """
1725:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1726:     (repo / "aaa.txt").write_text("readable, sorts first\n")
1727:     denied = repo / "bbb.txt"
1728:     denied.write_text("this one will not be readable\n")
1729:     mirror = tmp_path / "mirror"
1730:     _, ident = build_and_read(repo, mirror)
1731:     user = os.environ.get("USERNAME") or ""
1732:     if not user:
1733:         pytest.skip("USERNAME not set; cannot build a deny ACE")
1734:     deny = subprocess.run(["icacls", str(denied), "/deny", user + ":(RD)"],
1735:                           capture_output=True, text=True)
1736:     if deny.returncode != 0:
1737:         pytest.skip("icacls deny unavailable: " + deny.stdout + deny.stderr)
1738:     try:
1739:         proc = run_verify(repo, mirror, ident)
1740:         assert proc.returncode == 1, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1741:         assert "could not be read" in proc.stdout.lower(), (
1742:             "an unreadable input must be named as unreadable, not "
1743:             "reported as drift: " + proc.stdout)
1744:     finally:
1745:         undo = subprocess.run(["icacls", str(denied), "/remove:d", user],
1746:                               capture_output=True, text=True)
1747:         assert undo.returncode == 0, undo.stdout + undo.stderr
1748:         assert denied.read_text(), "the deny ACE is still in force"
1749: 
2163: def test_a_link_target_that_is_itself_a_link_is_refused(tmp_path):
2164:     """Cross-vendor round 3: with repo/linked -> alias and alias -> t,
2165:     the walk records the target as spelled, `alias`, and a mirror path
2166:     at `t` neither passes through a link nor overlaps `alias` as text,
2167:     so the force switch could delete t. A followed target with a
2168:     reparse point at itself or among its ancestors is refused."""
2169:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
2170:     t = tmp_path / "t"
2171:     t.mkdir()
2172:     (t / "x.txt").write_text("real target\n")
2173:     alias = tmp_path / "alias"
2174:     make_junction(alias, t)
2175:     make_junction(repo / "linked", alias)
2176:     proc = run_mirror(repo, t, "-Force")
2177:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
2178:     assert "is itself a directory link or sits beneath one" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
2179:     assert (t / "x.txt").read_text() == "real target\n"
2180:

2182: def test_an_inner_link_target_is_protected(tmp_path):
2183:     """Cross-vendor round 2: with repo/outer -> A and A/inner -> B, a
2184:     guard over recorded (outer) links alone protects A and lets a mirror
2185:     path at B with the force switch delete B. Every target the walk
2186:     reaches is protected."""
2187:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
2188:     a = tmp_path / "a"
2189:     a.mkdir()
2190:     b = tmp_path / "b"
2191:     b.mkdir()
2192:     (b / "keep.txt").write_text("inner target\n")
2193:     make_junction(a / "inner", b)
2194:     make_junction(repo / "outer", a)
2195:     proc = run_mirror(repo, b, "-Force")
2196:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
2197:     assert "overlaps the target of a source link" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
2198:     assert (b / "keep.txt").read_text() == "inner target\n"
2199:

2201: def test_a_mirror_path_through_a_link_is_refused(tmp_path):
2202:     """Cross-vendor round 2: a text comparison cannot see that a mirror
2203:     path spelled through some other junction lands inside a protected
2204:     target. Any mirror or override path with a reparse point among its
2205:     existing ancestors is refused outright."""
2206:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
2207:     outside = tmp_path / "outside"
2208:     outside.mkdir()
2209:     (outside / "x.txt").write_text("linked\n")
2210:     make_junction(repo / "linked", outside)
2211:     alias = tmp_path / "alias"
2212:     make_junction(alias, outside)
2213:     proc = run_mirror(repo, alias / "mirror")
2214:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
2215:     assert "passes through a directory link" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
2216:     assert not (alias / "mirror").exists()
2217:     assert (outside / "x.txt").read_text() == "linked\n"
2218:     proc = run_mirror(repo, tmp_path / "mirror", "-OverrideOut", str(alias / "o.txt"))
2219:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
2220:     assert "passes through a directory link" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
2221:     assert not (outside / "o.txt").exists()

2290: def test_extra_inputs_are_covered_by_the_digest(tmp_path):
2291:     # Copied in as part of construction, so the identity record describes
2292:     # the tree the reviewer actually reads.
2293:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
2294:     extra = tmp_path / "standards.md"
2295:     extra.write_text("house rules\n")
2296:     mirror = tmp_path / "mirror"
2297:     proc, ident = build_and_read(repo, mirror, "-ExtraInput", str(extra))
2298:     assert (mirror / "standards.md").read_text() == "house rules\n"
2299:     assert "standards.md" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
2300:     ok = run_verify(repo, mirror, ident)
2301:     assert ok.returncode == 0, ok.stdout + ok.stderr
2302:     (mirror / "standards.md").write_text("tampered\n")
2303:     tampered = run_verify(repo, mirror, ident)
2304:     assert tampered.returncode == 1, tampered.stdout + tampered.stderr

2306: 
2307: def test_two_extra_inputs_with_one_leaf_name_are_refused(tmp_path):
2308:     """Cross-vendor round 2, 2026-09-01.
2309: 
2310:     The plan promises to copy in EACH named file. The implementation
2311:     flattened every input to its leaf name and wrote it with -Force, so
2312:     two inputs called `standards.md` silently became one: the record
2313:     listed a destination that could not show which of them arrived, and
2314:     the reviewer read a tree the record misdescribed. Refusing is the
2315:     honest option, because the mirror re-roots every path and there is
2316:     no correct second location that the plan ever froze.
2317:     """
2318:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
2319:     a = tmp_path / "a"; a.mkdir(); (a / "standards.md").write_text("first\n")
2320:     b = tmp_path / "b"; b.mkdir(); (b / "standards.md").write_text("second\n")
2321:     mirror = tmp_path / "mirror"
2322:     proc = run_mirror(repo, mirror, "-ExtraInput", str(a / "standards.md"),
2323:                       "-ExtraInput", str(b / "standards.md"))
2324:     # Assert the REFUSAL, not the exit code: run_mirror passes -SkipProbe,
2325:     # which blocks every build at the end anyway, so `returncode == 1`
2326:     # alone passes whether or not this case is handled at all. The first
2327:     # draft of this test did exactly that and passed against the defect.
2328:     assert "share the destination name" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
2329:     assert "standards.md" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
2330:     assert not (mirror / "standards.md").exists(), "copied one of them anyway"
2331: 
2332: 
2333: def test_an_extra_input_cannot_overwrite_a_file_the_mirror_already_has(tmp_path):
2334:     """Same round, the other half of the collision.
2335: 
2336:     An -ExtraInput landing on a name the copied tree already carries
2337:     replaces reviewed content with unreviewed content, and the digest
2338:     then certifies the replacement as the tree.
2339:     """

1524: def record_field(stdout, name):
1525:     """One scalar field of the construction record, or None."""
1526:     for line in stdout.splitlines():
1527:         if line.startswith(name + ": "):
1528:             return line[len(name) + 2:].strip()
1529:     return None
1530: 
1531: 
1532: def build_and_read(repo, mirror, *extra):
1533:     proc = run_mirror(repo, mirror, *extra)
1534:     assert_built(proc)
1535:     return proc, {
1536:         "source_head": record_field(proc.stdout, "source_head"),
1537:         "mirror_head": record_field(proc.stdout, "mirror_head"),
1538:         "source_status_sha256": record_field(proc.stdout,
1539:                                              "source_status_sha256"),
1540:         "mirror_state_sha256": record_field(proc.stdout,
1541:                                             "mirror_state_sha256"),
1542:         "mirror": record_field(proc.stdout, "mirror"),
1543:     }
1544: 
1545: 
1546: def run_verify(repo, mirror, ident, **override):
1547:     """`mirror` is BOTH the live -MirrorPath argument (2nd positional)
1548:     AND, by default, -ExpectedMirrorPath - the path the identity was
1549:     recorded at. Pass `mirror=` in `override` to point the two at
1550:     different places on purpose (test_verify_refuses_a_mirror_at_a_
1551:     different_path)."""
1552:     fields = dict(ident)
1553:     fields.update(override)
1554:     return subprocess.run(
1555:         [ps_host(), "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-File", str(MIRROR),
1556:          "-VerifyIdentity", "-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-MirrorPath", str(mirror),
1557:          "-ExpectedMirrorPath", str(fields["mirror"]),
1558:          "-SourceHead", str(fields["source_head"]),
1559:          "-MirrorHead", str(fields["mirror_head"]),
1560:          "-SourceStatusSha256", str(fields["source_status_sha256"]),
1561:          "-MirrorStateSha256", str(fields["mirror_state_sha256"])],
1562:         capture_output=True, text=True)
1563:

1567: def test_the_record_carries_both_heads_and_the_source_status_hash(tmp_path):
1568:     """The positive control for construction.
1569: 
1570:     `mirror_head` differs from `source_head` whenever remediation
1571:     committed, which is the ordinary case for a repo carrying a tracked
1572:     back-channel - so the two fields are not interchangeable and a
1573:     record printing one twice would be wrong in the common case.
1574:     """
1575:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1576:     proc, ident = build_and_read(repo, tmp_path / "mirror")
1577:     assert ident["source_head"], proc.stdout
1578:     assert ident["mirror_head"], proc.stdout
1579:     assert len(ident["source_status_sha256"]) == 64, proc.stdout
1580:     assert ident["source_head"] == git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").strip()

The plan needs further amendments before implementation. The most serious defects are deletion before destination validation and a reader whose size check does not bound its read. ([plan:193](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:193), [plan:237](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:237), [plan:552](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:552)) **Plan verdict: FIX the destination ordering, root handling, parser validation, reader bounds, and contradictory assurances identified below.**

1. The design explicitly calls fusion constructible, rejects it because the round remains exposed, and records the dependency from completed probe to wrapper body to preparation. The existing build invokes the probe before printing its record, and preparation installs the supplied body. ([design:38](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window-design.md:38), [design:50](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window-design.md:50), [tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1725](C:/Temp/pxr2/tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1725), [tools/dispatch-round.ps1:530](C:/Temp/pxr2/tools/dispatch-round.ps1:530)) **PASS.**

2. The listed lexical checks are present and precede copying, but `-Force` deletes the sidecar before the existing ancestor-link checks and before path-budget validation finishes. For example, with `C:\alias` pointing into the source repository, `-MirrorPath C:\alias\mirror -Force` can delete the source’s existing `mirror.source-manifest` before the later mirror-path guard refuses the junction. The override’s full protection also includes ancestor-link and followed-target checks beyond the blocks the plan copies. ([plan:193](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:193), [plan:237](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:237), [tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1258](C:/Temp/pxr2/tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1258), [tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1282](C:/Temp/pxr2/tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1282)) **FIX: finish all destination validation before removal, and explicitly include the sidecar in the applicable alias and protected-target checks.**

3. `CreateNew` closes the overwrite race at the final file component. Its guarantee does not extend to directory components: an ancestor replaced by a junction before the open can redirect creation into that junction’s target. The writer contains no protection against that redirection, and the existing ancestor checks occur earlier. ([plan:165](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:165), [plan:175](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:175), [tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1258](C:/Temp/pxr2/tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1258)) **FIX: state the final-component guarantee precisely and establish a protected parent-directory assumption or prevent ancestor replacement through creation.**

4. The root test is incorrect on both available hosts. Executing the proposed helper in memory returned `C:\` as the leaf of `C:\`, which misses the `^[A-Za-z]:$` condition and reaches `Join-Path` with an empty parent. For `\\server\share\`, Windows PowerShell 5.1 returned no leaf, while PowerShell 7 derived `\\server\share.source-manifest`, naming another share. Both call sites share the helper, but the helper does not implement its root contract. ([plan:37](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:37), [plan:147](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:147), [plan:200](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:200), [plan:538](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:538)) **FIX: detect roots using normalized filesystem-root semantics, including UNC roots, before leaf derivation; test both hosts.**

5. The ordinal dictionaries preserve distinct case-sensitive keys. In-memory execution on both hosts reported `File.txt` as leaving and `file.txt` as entering coverage with identical hashes. ([plan:466](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:466), [plan:486](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:486)) **PASS.**

6. Duplicate keys and missing separators are counted, but malformed records remain accepted. The parser accepts `bad.txt not-a-hash` and `empty.txt `, and silently skips whitespace-only records. Both hosts reproduced this behavior. A truncated final hash therefore becomes an ordinary difference without the promised incompleteness warning. ([plan:461](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:461), [plan:473](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:473), [plan:480](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:480)) **FIX: validate the hash field and record grammar, count invalid records, and distinguish the trailing split artifact from actual blank records.**

**S1. Class sweep**

The amended text contains these instances:

- **Destination safety exceeds the ordering and mechanism.** “Same destination guards,” “never write through … a link,” and placement outside both identities require the corrections in claims 2–3. ([plan:7](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:7), [plan:38](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:38), [design:82](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window-design.md:82)) **FIX: align those assurances with the completed destination protections.**
- **Root refusal, malformed-record reporting, byte bounds, and control-character rendering overstate their implementations.** These are the counterexamples in claims 4, 6, and 10. ([plan:37](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:37), [plan:461](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:461), [plan:503](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:503), [plan:549](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:549)) **FIX: implement the stated properties before retaining those assurances.**
- **The interface section retains the superseded behavior.** It promises `Appeared`/`Vanished` fields and says the explanation “returns nothing,” while the proposed implementation returns `Entered`/`Left` and emits pipeline objects. The design’s D2 paragraph also retains “appeared, vanished.” ([plan:306](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:306), [plan:496](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:496), [design:73](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window-design.md:73)) **FIX: synchronize the interface and D2 prose with the amended functions.**
- **The universal exit-code constraint contradicts the new build refusals.** An existing sidecar now produces exit 2, explicitly asserted by the new test, despite the constraint that every new path merely adds output before an existing exit 1. ([plan:16](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:16), [plan:79](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:79), [plan:229](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:229)) **FIX: distinguish preservation of verification verdicts from the intentionally added construction refusals.**
- **The new backlog text reintroduces the quota overstatement.** “Each such write costs one round” ignores writes reverted before measurement and refusals before client invocation. ([plan:819](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:819), [plan:665](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:665)) **FIX: describe persistent drift detected after client execution as the case that wastes a round.**
- **“Content of every path” omits the subject-selection qualification.** That wording appears in the amended contract and backlog despite the deliberate omission of deletion-only entries. ([plan:659](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:659), [plan:829](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:829), [tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:462](C:/Temp/pxr2/tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:462)) **FIX: describe status fields plus the selected content subjects, including recursive directory expansion.**
- **The planned tests do not establish the universal advisory assurance.** The focused commands omit the new destination-guard tests and the case-only, valid-hostile, and malformed-record tests because their names do not match the selectors. The later full-module run includes them, but the advertised intermediate verification does not. ([plan:79](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:79), [plan:118](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:118), [plan:392](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:392), [plan:442](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:442), [design:162](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window-design.md:162)) **FIX: correct the selectors and add regression cases for the concrete failures above; narrow what the tests claim to prove.**

**S2. What else could it be**

- **Collision with a declared extra input.** `-ExtraInput` is resolved before the new sidecar guard. If that input is the existing sidecar path and `-Force` is supplied, the amendment deletes the input before its eventual copy. The existing unchecked `Copy-Item` can then leave the input absent while construction proceeds. ([tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:919](C:/Temp/pxr2/tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:919), [plan:237](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:237), [tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1554](C:/Temp/pxr2/tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1554)) **FIX: refuse sidecar collisions with declared extra inputs before removal.**
- **Dangling links and ancestor redirection.** `Test-Path` is not a reliable existence test for dangling reparse points; the existing helper documents that exact limitation. The new guard uses it nevertheless. Ancestor redirection also exposes the early deletion and later creation described in claims 2–3. ([plan:223](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:223), [tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1227](C:/Temp/pxr2/tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1227)) **FIX: inspect entry attributes and finish alias validation before mutation.**
- **Reader redirection into unrelated files.** Verification accepts a leaf and follows it through `ReadAllBytes`; it does not reject a substituted file link. Because the parser accepts almost any line containing a space, ordinary text from a linked file can become printed “left manifest coverage” names. ([plan:544](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:544), [plan:562](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:562), [plan:474](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:474)) **FIX: define and enforce reader link handling and validate records before rendering them.**
- **Memory amplification within the nominal byte limit.** Splitting a 64 MiB newline-heavy file can produce tens of millions of array entries before the parser discards blanks. The byte array, decoded string, split result, dictionaries, and sorted groups coexist. The statement “far below a memory problem” has no corresponding working-memory bound. ([plan:549](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:549), [plan:564](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:564), [plan:467](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:467)) **FIX: bound record count and length and avoid materializing an unrestricted split.**
- **Nonterminating metadata failure bypasses the size test.** `Get-Item` lacks `-ErrorAction Stop`. A targeted in-memory probe on both hosts showed its error escaping as an error record while parsing continued. With no usable length, the intended size refusal is not established. The script ordinarily leaves error preference unset. ([plan:552](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:552), [tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:519](C:/Temp/pxr2/tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:519)) **FIX: make metadata failure enter the advisory fallback and enforce the actual read limit independently.**
- **Display manipulation survives the renderer.** Besides C1 controls, U+2028 and U+202E pass unchanged, allowing line-separator or bidirectional display manipulation. Successful forged records also receive no emitted advisory warning; the uncertainty appears only in the reference or failure-specific messages. ([plan:511](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:511), [plan:570](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:570), [plan:795](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:795)) **FIX: escape display-affecting Unicode characters and introduce every emitted comparison as unauthenticated advisory information.**
- **Host-dependent root behavior survives the amendment.** Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7 disagree on UNC leaves, and both defeat the proposed drive-root condition, as detailed in claim 4. ([plan:151](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:151)) **FIX: add explicit drive-root and UNC-root cases on both hosts.**

**Final check:** UNVERIFIED: the future implementation’s end-to-end and full-gate results, which the plan schedules in Task 5. They are not evidence for this verdict. ([plan:929](C:/Temp/pxr2/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-05-mirror-identity-window.md:929)) No instruction in a reviewed file caused a pause, declined claim, or change of task.
